# 讨董联盟解散后的历史群雄独立（2026-10-16，线程「讨董联盟解散后群雄势力」）
# 调用：hd_hb_spatial_cleanup_effect 末尾排 hd_hw_after_breakup；D30 兜底。只在 184 开局跑一次。
# 规则（boki）：首领已有公国或以上有地头衔且独立，则不割据；否则在史实地点生成，默认只拿一个郡。
# 不挤占：只动天子领内（及部族单县、封臣条目原定主君领内）的地；已独立者、朝贡国、先生成的群雄、玩家领内都不碰。
# 只拿太守位：郡头衔只从虚构郡守或无主处接，不带封臣；虚构人物的县夺取，天子直辖县也夺（天子首都除外），真实人物的县不夺，县级真实县主只收编。

hd_hw_run_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { exists = global_var:hd_hw_done }
			game_start_date = 190.1.1
			# 184 剧本流程重制（2026-10-22）：改为诛董时先于解体判定运行
			OR = {
				exists = global_var:hd_hb_started
				exists = global_var:hd184r_warlord_era
			}
		}
		set_global_variable = hd_hw_done
		hd_hw_mark_effect = yes
		hd_hw_spawn_all_effect = yes
		# 184 剧本流程重制 D24/D25/D39（2026-10-21）：按表补足相连郡、搬迁不在史实郡的已独立者、张鲁独立
		hd184r_hw_adjust_all_effect = yes
		hd_hw_alliances_effect = yes
		debug_log = "HD_HW RUN_COMPLETED"
	}
}

# 第一遍：全部清单首领加 hd_hw_leader，已成型的再加 hd_hw_keep；
# 虚构人物加 hd_hw_fict（部族县主另加 hd_hw_tribal），184 年虚构人物所持郡县的平民继承人同样补标。

	character:su_dai ?= { hd_hw_consider_effect = yes } # F17b 苏代（长沙，刘表封臣）
	character:zhang_lu ?= { hd_hw_consider_effect = yes } # F28a 张鲁（汉中，刘焉部）
	character:shi_xie ?= { hd_hw_consider_effect = yes } # F35a 士燮（交趾，朱符名义下）

hd_hw_consider_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_alive = no }
		debug_log = "HD_HW SKIP_DEAD"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = hd_hw_keep }
		debug_log = "HD_HW SKIP_ESTABLISHED"
	}
	else_if = {
		# 孙坚的 hds 链已让他出走或改为营地时不再生成。
		limit = {
			this = character:sun_jian
			OR = { has_character_flag = hds_sj_departed has_character_flag = hds_sj_camp_converted }
		}
		debug_log = "HD_HW SKIP_SUN_JIAN_CHAIN"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_imprisoned = yes imprisoner ?= { is_ai = no } }
		debug_log = "HD_HW SKIP_PLAYER_PRISONER"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_ai = no }
		add_character_flag = hd_hw_offer
		trigger_event = hd_hw.0001
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { hd_hw_player_above_trigger = yes }
		add_character_flag = hd_hw_offer
		save_scope_as = hd_hw_petitioner
		random_liege_or_above = {
			limit = { is_ai = no NOT = { title:h_china.holder ?= this } }
			trigger_event = hd_hw.0002
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_courtier = yes host ?= { is_ai = no NOT = { title:h_china.holder ?= this } } }
		add_character_flag = hd_hw_offer
		save_scope_as = hd_hw_petitioner
		host = { trigger_event = hd_hw.0002 }
	}
	else = { hd_hw_spawn_self_effect = yes }
}

# 第三遍：公孙范、田楷与公孙瓒结盟；因主君无效而独立的封臣条目与原定主君结盟。
hd_hw_alliances_effect = {
	character:gong_sun_fan ?= { hd_hw_ally_effect = { ALLY = character:gong_sun_zan } }
	character:tian_kai ?= { hd_hw_ally_effect = { ALLY = character:gong_sun_zan } }
	character:runan_chen_yi ?= { if = { limit = { exists = var:hd_hw_ally } hd_hw_ally_effect = { ALLY = var:hd_hw_ally } } }
	character:yuan_yi ?= { if = { limit = { exists = var:hd_hw_ally } hd_hw_ally_effect = { ALLY = var:hd_hw_ally } } }
	character:sun_jian ?= { if = { limit = { exists = var:hd_hw_ally } hd_hw_ally_effect = { ALLY = var:hd_hw_ally } } }
	character:huang_zu ?= { if = { limit = { exists = var:hd_hw_ally } hd_hw_ally_effect = { ALLY = var:hd_hw_ally } } }
	character:su_dai ?= { if = { limit = { exists = var:hd_hw_ally } hd_hw_ally_effect = { ALLY = var:hd_hw_ally } } }
	character:zhang_lu ?= { if = { limit = { exists = var:hd_hw_ally } hd_hw_ally_effect = { ALLY = var:hd_hw_ally } } }
	character:shi_xie ?= { if = { limit = { exists = var:hd_hw_ally } hd_hw_ally_effect = { ALLY = var:hd_hw_ally } } }
}

hd_hw_ally_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_character_flag = hd_hw_keep
			is_alive = yes
			is_independent_ruler = yes
			exists = $ALLY$
			$ALLY$ = { is_alive = yes is_landed = yes is_independent_ruler = yes }
			NOT = { this = $ALLY$ }
			NOT = { is_allied_to = $ALLY$ }
			NOT = { is_at_war_with = $ALLY$ }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes $ALLY$ = { is_ai = yes } }
			save_temporary_scope_as = hd_hw_ally_owner
			$ALLY$ = { save_temporary_scope_as = hd_hw_ally_target }
			create_alliance = {
				target = scope:hd_hw_ally_target
				allied_through_owner = scope:hd_hw_ally_owner
				allied_through_target = scope:hd_hw_ally_target
			}
			debug_log = "HD_HW ALLIED"
		}
		else = { debug_log = "HD_HW ALLY_SKIPPED_PLAYER" }
	}
}

		else_if = { limit = { title:d_wuping = { hd_hw_duchy_owned_trigger = yes } } title:d_wuping = { save_scope_as = hd_hw_duchy } }

		else_if = { limit = { title:d_jiuzhen = { hd_hw_duchy_usable_trigger = yes } } title:d_jiuzhen = { save_scope_as = hd_hw_duchy } }
		else_if = { limit = { title:d_dianbian = { hd_hw_duchy_usable_trigger = yes } } title:d_dianbian = { save_scope_as = hd_hw_duchy } }
		else_if = { limit = { title:d_hepu_jiaozhou = { hd_hw_duchy_usable_trigger = yes } } title:d_hepu_jiaozhou = { save_scope_as = hd_hw_duchy } }
		else_if = { limit = { title:d_wuping = { hd_hw_duchy_usable_trigger = yes } } title:d_wuping = { save_scope_as = hd_hw_duchy } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:hd_hw_duchy }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:hd_hw_seat } }
			if = {
				limit = { scope:hd_hw_duchy = { tier = tier_county } }
				scope:hd_hw_duchy = { save_scope_as = hd_hw_seat }
			}
			else = { scope:hd_hw_duchy.title_capital_county = { save_scope_as = hd_hw_seat } }
		}
		hd_hw_apply_effect = yes
	}
	else = { hd_hw_no_land_effect = yes }
}

hd_hw_no_land_effect = {
	debug_log = "HD_HW NO_LAND"
	debug_log_scopes = yes
	# 公孙范、田楷拿不到地且本人无地时，作公孙瓒手下的无地将领。
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { this = character:gong_sun_fan this = character:tian_kai }
			is_ai = yes
			is_landed = no
			character:gong_sun_zan ?= { is_alive = yes is_ai = yes is_landed = yes }
		}
		character:gong_sun_zan = { add_courtier = scope:hd_hw_actor }
		debug_log = "HD_HW JOINED_GONGSUN_ZAN"
	}
}

hd_hw_apply_effect = {
	scope:hd_hw_duchy = { save_scope_as = hd_hw_cand }
	# 接收郡头衔与收编县主的人：封臣条目的主君只是公级时归主君，否则归首领本人。
	if = {
		limit = {
			hd_hw_home_ok_trigger = yes
			NOT = { scope:hd_hw_home_liege = { any_held_title = { tier >= tier_kingdom is_landless_type_title = no } } }
		}
		scope:hd_hw_home_liege = { save_scope_as = hd_hw_recv }
	}
	else = { save_scope_as = hd_hw_recv }
	# 天子失去最后一个县时不能消亡（同 hd_dong_grant_duchy_effect）。
	title:h_china = { if = { limit = { exists = holder } set_landless_title = yes } }

	# 一、郡头衔（太守位）：不带封臣转给接收人。
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:hd_hw_duchy = {
				hd_hw_duchy_takeable_trigger = yes
				NOT = { holder ?= scope:hd_hw_recv }
			}
		}
		create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = granted save_scope_as = hd_hw_change_a add_claim_on_loss = no }
		scope:hd_hw_duchy = { change_title_holder = { holder = scope:hd_hw_recv change = scope:hd_hw_change_a } }
		resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:hd_hw_change_a
	}

	# 二、治所县：首领在本区一县皆无时，拿治所县，拿不到就拿本区第一个可夺的县。
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_held_title = { tier = tier_county hd_hw_in_region_trigger = yes } } }
		if = {
			limit = { scope:hd_hw_seat = { hd_hw_grab_county_trigger = yes } }
			scope:hd_hw_seat = { save_scope_as = hd_hw_pick }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { scope:hd_hw_duchy = { tier = tier_duchy } }
			scope:hd_hw_duchy = {
				random_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
					limit = {
						tier = tier_county
						hd_hw_grab_county_trigger = yes
					}
					save_scope_as = hd_hw_pick
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:hd_hw_pick }
			create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = conquest save_scope_as = hd_hw_change_b add_claim_on_loss = no }
			scope:hd_hw_pick = { change_title_holder_include_vassals = { holder = scope:hd_hw_actor change = scope:hd_hw_change_b } }
			resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:hd_hw_change_b
		}
	}

	# 三、本区其余县：虚构人物和天子的县夺归首领；真实县主（县级、名下县全在本区）收编给接收人，不夺地。
	# 临时列表在整次运行中不清空，故每次遍历都带本区限制。
	if = {
		limit = { scope:hd_hw_duchy = { tier = tier_duchy } }
		create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = conquest save_scope_as = hd_hw_change_c add_claim_on_loss = no }
		scope:hd_hw_duchy = {
			every_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
				limit = { tier = tier_county hd_hw_grab_county_trigger = yes }
				change_title_holder_include_vassals = { holder = scope:hd_hw_actor change = scope:hd_hw_change_c }
			}
		}
		resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:hd_hw_change_c
		if = {
			limit = { scope:hd_hw_recv = { any_held_title = { tier >= tier_duchy is_landless_type_title = no } } }
			scope:hd_hw_duchy = {
				every_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
					limit = {
						tier = tier_county
						holder ?= {
							hd_hw_vassalize_holder_trigger = yes
							NOT = { is_in_list = hd_hw_vassalize }
						}
					}
					holder = { add_to_temporary_list = hd_hw_vassalize }
				}
			}
			create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = swear_fealty save_scope_as = hd_hw_change_d add_claim_on_loss = no }
			every_in_list = {
				list = hd_hw_vassalize
				limit = { hd_hw_vassalize_holder_trigger = yes }
				change_liege = { liege = scope:hd_hw_recv change = scope:hd_hw_change_d }
			}
			resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:hd_hw_change_d
		}
	}

	hd_hw_finish_effect = yes
}

hd_hw_finish_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				any_held_title = { tier = tier_county hd_hw_in_region_trigger = yes }
				any_vassal_or_below = { any_held_title = { tier = tier_county hd_hw_in_region_trigger = yes } }
			}
		}
		# 封臣条目：主君有效、且本人有地等级低于主君时宣誓效忠；否则独立并记下结盟对象。
		if = {
			limit = {
				hd_hw_home_ok_trigger = yes
				OR = {
					AND = {
						scope:hd_hw_home_liege = { any_held_title = { tier >= tier_kingdom is_landless_type_title = no } }
						NOT = { any_held_title = { tier >= tier_kingdom is_landless_type_title = no } }
					}
					AND = {
						scope:hd_hw_home_liege = { any_held_title = { tier >= tier_duchy is_landless_type_title = no } }
						NOT = { any_held_title = { tier >= tier_duchy is_landless_type_title = no } }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { liege ?= scope:hd_hw_home_liege } }
				create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = swear_fealty save_scope_as = hd_hw_change_e add_claim_on_loss = no }
				change_liege = { liege = scope:hd_hw_home_liege change = scope:hd_hw_change_e }
				resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:hd_hw_change_e
			}
			hd_force_celestial_effect = yes
			debug_log = "HD_HW SPAWNED_AS_VASSAL"
		}
		else = {
			hd_force_independent_celestial_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:hd_hw_home_liege }
				set_variable = { name = hd_hw_ally value = scope:hd_hw_home_liege }
			}
			debug_log = "HD_HW SPAWNED_INDEPENDENT"
		}
		# 首都不在本区时移到本区实际持有的县，优先治所县。
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { capital_county ?= { hd_hw_in_region_trigger = yes } } }
			clear_saved_scope = hd_hw_pick
			if = {
				limit = { scope:hd_hw_seat = { holder ?= scope:hd_hw_actor } }
				scope:hd_hw_seat = { save_scope_as = hd_hw_pick }
			}
			else = {
				random_held_title = {
					limit = { tier = tier_county hd_hw_in_region_trigger = yes }
					save_scope_as = hd_hw_pick
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:hd_hw_pick }
				set_realm_capital = scope:hd_hw_pick
				set_location = { location = scope:hd_hw_pick.title_province stick_to_location = yes }
				domicile ?= { move_domicile = scope:hd_hw_pick.title_province }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_held_title = { tier = tier_county is_landless_type_title = no NOT = { hd_hw_in_region_trigger = yes } } }
			debug_log = "HD_HW EXCLAVE"
			debug_log_scopes = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { is_imprisoned = yes } release_from_prison = yes }
		add_character_flag = hd_hw_keep
		debug_log_scopes = yes
	}
	else = { hd_hw_no_land_effect = yes }
	clear_saved_scope = hd_hw_home_liege
	clear_saved_scope = hd_hw_duchy
	clear_saved_scope = hd_hw_seat
	clear_saved_scope = hd_hw_pick
}
